
MONOPOLY-LK Simulation

Player 1 : Aggressive Invester
Player 2 : Conservative Banker
Player 3 : Risk Taker
Player 4 : Oppertunistic Trader

Each Player begins with LKR 30,000.

Aggressive Invester rolls 5.
Conservative Banker rolls 4.
Risk Taker rolls 7.
Oppertunistic Trader rolls 7.

There is a tie between the following players:
- Risk Taker
- Oppertunistic Trader
will roll again

Risk Taker rolls 8.
Oppertunistic Trader rolls 7.

Risk Taker will begin the game.

Turn Order:

Oppertunistic Trader
Aggressive Invester
Conservative Banker
